Frontier-arbiter rule simulation — 1489 stored turns from 4 duels (dropped {'lt3_frontier': 70, 'no_greedy': 18, 'no_echo': 23}); frontier = engy glm-5.2 (3 samples @T0.8 + greedy); teacher echoes only, tau=0.03
new-attack echo subset: 203 turns, 8130 Engy teacher echoes (qwen3.8-27b, canonical rendering), $2.07 (7426 echoes / $1.89 recorded by completed runs; the rest priced at that rate) (honest king re-echoed on the same turns so attack comparisons are same-source)

DEFINITIONS
  gate <method>/<F>: F disagrees when its action differs from ALL k teacher refs — exact (norm_action), jacθ (best token-Jaccard < θ),
    surθ (lpC(y_F|∅) < min_i lpC(y_C^i|∅) − θ per byte); F = G (one greedy sample) or S (3 samples @T0.8, strict majority must disagree).
  agree branch = live min(R,G), band_c 4.0 (live_c2 = the stored era's 2.0); disagree branch = the alt:
    act:<sim>:<s> = [mean_j sim(y_A,y_F^j) − mean_ij sim(y_C^i,y_F^j)] × s × sd(live turn scores of the dialect); sim jaccard or exact (0/1)
    RF = centered tempered LME_j[lpC(y_F^j|z_A) − lpC(y_F^j|∅)]; V = mean_j f_j − mean_i a_i; AF = LME_j[lpC(y_A|z_F^j) − lpC(y_A|∅)]
    minRFG = min(RF, G); minVG = min(V, G). Echo-space alts always use the 3 sampled F refs; act uses the gate's F set.
  |sd = every leg standardised per dialect by (mu, sd) of the TEACHER's own leave-one-out values (R, RF, V, act; AF anchored on king+challenger,
    no teacher_own echo exists); thought leg = 2 − |m − mean(t_i)|/sigma_t with sigma_t = pooled within-turn sd of t_i per dialect.
    No cross echoes lpC(y_C^i|z_C^j), i≠j, exist in this data, so the anchor is the teacher's ref-0 thought on refs 1..2 (one LOO value per turn).
  wB_<mode>/<F> = variant B: teacher refs weighted by mean Jaccard to the F set (softmax at T or hard 1[sim≥0.5]); R = weighted centered LME, G from weighted mu/sd.
  HEADROOM (leave-one-out: ref 0 and F sample 0 dropped everywhere, so every family is scored on k−1=2 teacher refs and 2 F refs):
    mean turn score per family; z(F−T) = paired mean(F_own − T_own)/SE; z(T−K) likewise; dis% = disagree-branch rate on the LOO gate.
  NOISE (full refs, live pairing): margin = mean(chal − king); SE = sd/√n; z = margin/SE; bSE/live = bootstrap SE ÷ that of the same-units
    live rule (live_c4 raw, live_sd sd); |z|/|z0| = |z| ÷ |z of live_c4| (how much of the live separation of these losing challengers survives);
    rho = Spearman of per-turn (chal − king) vs live_c4; dis% = gate rate; varD = share of Σ(d−d̄)² carried by disagree turns.
    minAG = min(act:jaccard:1, G), minAFG = min(AF, G): G (or typ) still bounds the disagree branch.
  ATTACKS (king's thought or action replaced): ≤king% = turns where attack ≤ honest king (ties count); harmless if ≥ 85%. '=' = exact-tie share.
    filler/generic/parrot(sampled y_F^0 pasted): stored swarm echoes, all turns. fparrotG (greedy y_F pasted into the thought), copyF (action := greedy y_F),
    lscmd/catcmd (action := `ls -la` / `cat <first file in prefix>`, bash only): Engy echoes on the subset; copyF/ls/cat on ALL turns for action-space alts.
